\title{Technoanatomy:\\ \large Civilizational Survival as a Sign, Not a Size}
\author{Ajax Benander\thanks{Email:
\href{mailto:akb9408@rit.edu}{akb9408@rit.edu}. This paper develops the
existential-risk consequences of a survivorship model proved in a companion in
applied probability \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}, with the target-conditioning
machinery owned by the theorem companion \cite{GoldenTargeted}; both are
cited and restated, neither presupposed, and no downstream paper is
cited. Drafting
collaboration: Anthropic Claude (Opus~4.x, Fable~5), under the author's
direction.}}
\date{November 2026 --- Working Draft, rev.~4}
% R943 (Opus 5.5, Nov 08 2026), at the author's direction: the current version date set to November 2026 (his request of Nov 8).
% was: \date{September 2026 --- Working Draft, rev.~4}
\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}
Existential-risk practice manages a quantity that is insufficient by itself. Its ledgers price events, its progress measures index accumulated capacity, and both are silent on the variable a survivorship model proves decisive: technoanatomy, the structure that carries a civilization's capacity, read through scalars whose growth must outrun the capacity they carry, so that what decides is the sign of that race. Capability magnitude cannot substitute for favorable anatomy, viability requiring technoanatomy that outruns the strength together with favorable driver dynamics and a prerequisite schedule actually cleared. On the schedule model entered here, whose leak law is stipulated, the sign is being settled now at the highest leverage in the record, and conditioning on survival prefers that it be settled early. And no event of bounded duration certifies which path law generated the record, which does not by itself imply inability to identify an anatomy parameter through other measurements. Four consequences are drawn. The metric critique: an index of capability ranks worlds wrong in both directions, and the Kardashev scale measures visibility, not viability. The schedule: a third hazard channel, the gated sequence of prerequisites a civilization must land, is formalized as a discounted branching process feeding the survivorship model, with the emergence theorem, that survival conditioning selects any fractional lowering of the total hazard however rare the structure that buys it, and a survivorship-prefers-early-change result under stated monotone-coupling conditions; every consequence that depends on the stipulated leak law is carried at that grade. The rival periodization: the Precipice is engaged and three deltas are stated, in the quantity, the shape of time, and the endpoint. And the two errors about the human role, extinctionism and techno-optimism, refuted as mirror misreadings of one theorem. The ceiling's institutional reading closes the paper: a governance built for the regime where all the survivorship structure lives, with humility in the objective rather than in the disclaimer, and no certified safety anywhere.

Its ledgers price events, its progress measures index accumulated capacity, and both are silent on the variable a survivorship model proves decisive: the sign of what added capacity does to a civilization's internal hazard, called technoanatomy. Capability magnitude cannot substitute for favorable anatomy, viability requiring favorable anatomy together with favorable driver dynamics and a prerequisite schedule actually cleared. On the schedule model entered here, whose leak law is stipulated, the sign is being settled now at the highest leverage in the record, and conditioning on survival prefers that it be settled early. And no event of bounded duration certifies which path law generated the record, which does not by itself imply inability to identify an anatomy parameter through other measurements. Four consequences are drawn. The metric critique: an index of capability ranks worlds wrong in both directions, and the Kardashev scale measures visibility, not viability. The schedule: a third hazard channel, the gated sequence of prerequisites a civilization must land, is formalized as a discounted branching process feeding the survivorship model, with the emergence theorem, that survival conditioning selects any fractional lowering of the total hazard however rare the structure that buys it, and a survivorship-prefers-early-change result under stated monotone-coupling conditions; every consequence that depends on the stipulated leak law is carried at that grade. The rival periodization: the Precipice is engaged and three deltas are stated, in the quantity, the shape of time, and the endpoint. And the two errors about the human role, extinctionism and techno-optimism, refuted as mirror misreadings of one theorem. The ceiling's institutional reading closes the paper: a governance built for the regime where all the survivorship structure lives, with humility in the objective rather than in the disclaimer, and no certified safety anywhere.

\section{Introduction}\label{sec:intro}

The existential-risk field has, in a generation, built a serious
literature on how a civilization might end. It has not built an account
of what decides whether one can last, and the gap is not one of effort
but of quantity. The field's instruments price events: the probability
of a pandemic, a war, an unaligned system, summed and discounted into a
risk this century. Its progress measures index accumulated capacity:
energy captured, computation available, the Kardashev rungs. This paper
argues, from a theorem proved in a companion, that both instruments are
silent on the variable that decides survival, and that the silence is
structural rather than a matter of calibration. A civilization's added
capacity does two things at once: it muffles the hazards arriving from
outside, and it feeds a channel of hazard that arises from inside.
Whether indefinite survival is possible turns on whether the structure
that carries the capacity keeps pace with it, the sign of the race that
decides the second effect, and not on the size of the first; and an
index of capacity cannot see the sign, because the sign is not a
function of what the index measures.
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the decisive variable read as the race (R1064).
% was: Whether indefinite survival is possible turns on the sign of the second
% was: effect, not on the size of the first, and an index of capacity cannot
% was: see the sign because the sign is not a function of what the index
% was: measures.

\emph{The claim, in one sentence.} Capability magnitude cannot
substitute for favorable anatomy: viability requires technoanatomy that
outruns the strength together with favorable driver dynamics and a prerequisite
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the claim in unit form (R1064).
% was: substitute for favorable anatomy: viability requires a positive response
% was: sign together with favorable driver dynamics and a prerequisite
schedule that is cleared, none of which a capacity index measures; on
the schedule model entered here, whose leak law is stipulated, the sign
is being settled now at the highest leverage in the record, conditioning
on survival prefers that it be settled early, and past a survival-time
threshold the same conditioning prefers the schedule pushed, tilted
toward better completion states; and no event of bounded duration certifies which path law generated the record, which is the narrower claim the certification limit supports.

\emph{What is imported.} The companion in applied probability
\cite{GoldenSurvivorship} proves the two-driver theorem on which the claim rests,
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the imported theorem renamed (R1063).
% was: \cite{GoldenSurvivorship} proves the two-condition theorem on which the claim rests,
the model it is stated in, and the ceiling theorem that no finite
observation of a surviving path distinguishes the survivorship-conditioned
law from the unconditioned one. The theorem companion \cite{GoldenTargeted} supplies the general
machinery for records drawn under target-conditioned laws, used here
only where a result consumes it directly. The target this paper works with is the survivorship companion's own
viability event, the state past which the external channel is driven to
zero; no Golden-specific object is imported. A downstream companion on
the Route World hypothesis, not cited here so that the dependency runs
one way, gives that target its natural-historical reading and asks
whether it explains our record; this paper discusses that reading only
in its closing section. Each import is restated in
Section~\ref{sec:imports} at its grade; nothing later depends on more
than is stated there.
\emph{Position in the branch.} The Golden branch is four papers in a
strict order of import, and the citation graph runs the same way as the
logic. The survivorship companion \cite{GoldenSurvivorship} is the root and owns the
hazard model, the viability theorem, the half-time theorem, and the
hard-wall and soft-wall conditioned laws; the theorem companion
\cite{GoldenTargeted} owns the general result on records drawn under
target-conditioned laws and the model-comparison machinery, and imports
only the survivorship companion; this paper imports both and owns the
strength/anatomy distinction, the prerequisite schedule, and the
mechanism and policy consequences; and a terminal Route World companion
imports all three to state the hypothesis that the survivorship target
explains our record, citing this paper as a possible mechanism without
this paper citing it. Nothing here is offered as support for that
hypothesis, and nothing here assumes it.

\emph{What is new.} The reading of the viability theorem as a statement
about two state variables, strength and anatomy
(Section~\ref{sec:coords}), with the analysis of a technoanatomy carried by several units, in which the weakest unit decides (Proposition~\ref{gr:prop:zeromark}). The schedule channel
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: what is new includes the unit analysis (R1064).
% was: (Section~\ref{sec:coords}). The schedule channel
(Section~\ref{sec:schedule}): the sequence of gated prerequisites a
civilization must land, formalized as a discounted branching process
with log-spread rates, its derailment hazard, its modern-era term acting
on the anatomy rather than on the capacity, the surviving-completion
kernel through which it feeds the survivorship model, the survival-time
threshold past which conditioning switches from favoring the unpushed
schedule to favoring the pushed one, tilted toward better completion states, the emergence theorem, which generalizes that threshold to any fractional drop in the total hazard of departure, however small and however rare the structure that buys it (Theorem~\ref{sch:thm:emergence}), and the front-loading
% R962 (Opus 5.5, Nov 11 2026), at the author's direction: author-attribution metacommentary set impersonal.
% was: schedule to favoring the pushed one, tilted toward better completion states, the author's emergence theorem, which generalizes that threshold to any fractional drop in the total hazard of departure, however small and however rare the structure that buys it (Theorem~\ref{sch:thm:emergence}), and the front-loading
% R876 (Opus 5.5, Oct 27 2026), at the author's direction: the emergence theorem cross-referenced.
result under stated monotone-coupling conditions. The two applications
(Sections~\ref{sec:refutes} and \ref{sec:precipice}): the Kardashev
scale as the cleanest case of strength against anatomy, and the
Precipice as the cleanest comparison with existential-risk practice,
with three deltas stated. The remaining consequences are collected as a
discussion (Section~\ref{sec:discussion}).

\emph{What is refused.} No certified safety, anywhere, including the
paper's own favorable conclusions; no claim that the sign has been
settled favorably; no occupant of the decisive moment, which the
prefers-early result shows is felt at every moment. These are stated once
in Section~\ref{sec:fences}.

\emph{Grades.} [P] proved here or in a companion; [S] scaling-level or
structural, argued without proof; [$\Phi$] a stipulative commitment; [O]
open, with its specification.

\section{Vocabulary}\label{sec:vocab}
\begin{itemize}[itemsep=2pt]
\item \textbf{Technostrength} $x^+$: accumulated capacity, the positive
part of the integral of the log-derivative driver; the quantity progress
indices measure.
\item \textbf{Technoanatomy}: the structure that carries capacity, taken
whole with its tendencies; read through scalars $\tau>0$, each the scale
of technostrength a unit of the structure can bear, carried by anatomy
rates; its sign, the sign of the race between scalar and strength, is the
decisive variable.
\item \textbf{Escape factor}: the driver's probability of escaping to
infinity; the strength's necessary condition, blind to the technoanatomy.
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the vocabulary for the unit form (R1064).
% was: \item \textbf{Technostrength} $x$: accumulated capacity, the integral of
% was: the log-derivative driver; the quantity progress indices measure.
% was: \item \textbf{Technoanatomy} $\rho$: the sign of what added capacity does

conditioning selects any fractional lowering of the total hazard,

\item \textbf{Schedule}: the gated sequence of prerequisites, each a race
among competing clocks, that a civilization must land.
\item \textbf{Gate, clock, leak}: one prerequisite; one candidate route
through it; the decay of capacity that incompleteness imposes.
\item \textbf{Derailment hazard} $\sigma_t$: the rate at which a wrong
clock fires first at the open gate; the schedule's contribution to the
hazard of Departure.
\item \textbf{Anatomy-directed gate}: a gate whose resolution acts on
the anatomy rate rather than on $x$; the modern era's kind.

mass of the anatomy rate has largely moved; not a freezing.

\item \textbf{Leverage}: the sensitivity of the hazard to the scalar,
which scales with the load and so with $x$; why the anatomy was
invisible in the deep past.

\item \textbf{The ceiling}: the imported theorem that no finite
observation certifies which measure a surviving record is under.
\item \textbf{Live third term}: the hypothesis, entered on evidence, that
the schedule is not yet exhausted.
\end{itemize}

\section{Imported results}\label{sec:imports}

\begin{definition}[The model; from \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:model}
The total hazard is
\[
h_t=B_t\operatorname{sech}^2x_t^++\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2(x_t^+/\tau_{j,t}):
\]
an external background $B$ muffled by the technostrength $x^+$, the
positive part of the accumulated capacity $x_t=\int_0^t\mu$, from its full
value at zero strength toward zero, plus an internal channel that is a
sum of units, each of amplitude $\Gamma_j$ and loaded by the strength in
proportion to $x^+/\tau_j$, where the technoanatomy scalar
$\tau_{j,t}=e^{y_{j,t}}$ is carried by an anatomy rate,
$y_{j,t}=y_{j,0}+\int_0^t\eta_j$. The noise sits on the rates: the driver
$\mu$ is Brownian, so that in the bare model the noise sits on
$-\frac{d}{dt}\log h$, and each anatomy rate carries its own. Departure is
the killing time $T$; viability is the event $\{\Lambda_\infty<\infty\}$
that the total hazard ever accrued stays finite.
\end{definition}
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the imported model in unit form (the survivorship companion's unit form imported (R1063, his direction of Nov 26): the internal channel a sum of units sinh^2(technostrength/technoanatomy scalar), the scalar an extraction from a technoanatomy taken whole with its tendency, the noise on the tendency by the Lindy argument, the external channel muffled by sech^2 of the technostrength, the technostrength the positive part of the credit; and his Nov 27 point that a very good technoanatomy couples to the strength, its scalar far ahead and still improving).
% was: \begin{definition}[The model; from \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:model}
% was: The total hazard is $h_t=B_t\varsigma_b(x_t)+\Gamma e^{-\rho x_t}$: an
% was: external background $B$ muffled by a bounded logistic factor of the
% was: accumulated capacity $x_t=\int_0^t\mu$, plus an internal term the same
% was: capacity feeds or starves according to the sign of the response exponent
% was: $\rho$; the driver $\mu$ is Brownian, so the noise sits on
% was: $-\frac{d}{dt}\log h$. Departure is the killing time $T$; viability is
% was: the event $\{\Lambda_\infty<\infty\}$ that the total hazard ever accrued
% was: stays finite.
% was: \end{definition}

\begin{definition}[The viability target; from \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:goldenpoint}
The target is the survivorship companion's viability event, the state
past which the external channel is driven to zero and the total hazard
ever accrued stays finite; its complement is Departure. The names
``Golden Point'' and ``Departure Point'' used below are labels for these
two objects and carry no content beyond them here; the construction of
the Departure Point from the record's side, and the hypothesis that our
record is drawn under conditioning on this target, belong to the
downstream Route World companion and are not assumed.
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Two drivers; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]
\label{imp:twocond}
(i) Technoanatomy that does not keep growing is fatal: if some unit's
scalar stays bounded, or its anatomy rate is driftless Brownian or
positive recurrent with nonpositive mean, viability is null for every
driver; and with the scalars fixed and the background frozen,
$\PP(T>t)\le e^{-h_\ast t}$ for the positive floor $h_\ast$ of the hazard.
(ii) Where the credit grows at least linearly, viability holds exactly
when $\int_0^\infty\sinh^2(x_t^+/\tau_{j,t})\,dt$ is finite for every unit:
the weakest unit decides. (iii) For anatomy rates independent of the
driver and of one another, and a driver of bounded drift,
$\PP(\Lambda_\infty<\infty)=V_{\mathrm{drv}}(\mu_0)\prod_jV_{\mathrm{anat}}(\eta_{j,0})$,
with $V_{\mathrm{drv}}$ the driver-viability functional, stated piecewise:
the scale-function escape probability for a transient driver, the
indicator of a positive stationary mean for a positive recurrent one,
zero for the Brownian driver, and open for general null recurrence.
$V_{\mathrm{anat}}$ is the same functional of an anatomy rate, and for a
constant rate, the degenerate case to which the companion's proof
applies verbatim, it is the indicator $\one\{\eta>0\}$. (iv) A
technoanatomy coupled to the strength through its lead,
$\tau_j=x^+e^{\ell_j}$ with the lead's rate carrying the noise, needs no
bound on the driver, the technoanatomy factors being the same functional
$V_{\mathrm{lead}}$ of the lead rates. (v) A technoanatomy \emph{locked}
to its strength, its lead a function of the strength alone, has no
tendency of its own. Writing $\rho$ for a unit's \emph{gain}, its hazard
reduction per unit of strength gained,
$-d\log h^{\mathrm{unit}}/dx$ along the history, a lead
$\ell_0+\tfrac12\rho x$ makes the unit the exponential
$\Gamma e^{-2\ell_0}e^{-\rho x}$ at small load, and the technoanatomy
factor degenerates to the indicator $\one\{\rho>0\}$; a lead held
constant has $\rho=0$, a scalar held constant has
$\rho\to-2/\bar\tau$, and both are fatal. The factors are logically distinct
% R1068 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the imported theorem carries the companion's locked case (R1068 there): the exponential internal channel as a technoanatomy locked to its strength at a constant gain.
% was: $V_{\mathrm{lead}}$ of the lead rates. The factors are logically distinct
necessary conditions: the first is the strength outrunning the
background, the others are the technoanatomy outrunning the strength, and
no index of accumulated capacity distinguishes the ascent that arrives
from the ascent that is a fall.
\end{theorem}
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the imported viability theorem with two drivers and its lead form (the survivorship companion's unit form imported (R1063, his direction of Nov 26): the internal channel a sum of units sinh^2(technostrength/technoanatomy scalar), the scalar an extraction from a technoanatomy taken whole with its tendency, the noise on the tendency by the Lindy argument, the external channel muffled by sech^2 of the technostrength, the technostrength the positive part of the credit; and his Nov 27 point that a very good technoanatomy couples to the strength, its scalar far ahead and still improving).

% was: \label{imp:twocond}
% was: For $\rho\le0$ viability is null for every parameter value and every
% was: driver. For $\rho>0$,
% was: $\PP(\Lambda_\infty<\infty)=\one\{\rho>0\}\,V_{\mathrm{drv}}(\mu_0)$, with
% was: $V_{\mathrm{drv}}$ the driver-viability functional, stated piecewise:
% was: the scale-function escape probability for a transient driver, the
% was: indicator of a positive stationary mean for a positive recurrent one,
% was: zero for the Brownian driver, and open for general null recurrence. The
% was: two factors are logically distinct necessary conditions, not independent
% was: events: the first is a sign and the second is silent on it. No index of
% was: accumulated capacity distinguishes the ascent that arrives from the

% was: \end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Adverse credit occupies half of logarithmic time in the
critical regime; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:half}
For the driftless driver, the set of times at which the credit sits below
its initial value has logarithmic density exactly one half, almost
surely, for every initial datum; the statement is confined to the
critical recurrent regime and fails for any driver whose credit is
transient.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proposition}[Trend survivorship; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:trend}
For a driftless Brownian rate, a stretch of travel in one direction that
has lasted an age $a$ since the rate's last zero lasts a further $s$ with
probability $(a/(a+s))^{1/2}$, exactly and for every volatility. This is
the Lindy law of a memoryless lifetime of unknown rate under the weight
$\lambda^{-1/2}d\lambda$, with the weight derived: the hazard of the
travel reversing is $1/(2a)$, the median further duration $3a$, the mean
infinite. It holds of the strength's rate, of an anatomy rate and of a
lead rate alike, and the driftless Brownian rate is the one random rate
with continuous paths, stationary independent increments and no
timescale. With a timescale the law is held only up to it: for the rate
$d\mu=-\nu\mu\,dt+\sigma\,dW$ the probability is
$\bigl((1-e^{-2\nu a})/(e^{2\nu s}-e^{-2\nu a})\bigr)^{1/2}$, which for a
damped rate, $\nu>0$, is the Lindy law for durations short against $1/\nu$
and memoryless beyond, and for a self-reinforcing rate, $\nu<0$, leaves a
tendency of age $a$ never reversing with probability
$(1-e^{-2|\nu|a})^{1/2}$.
% R1069 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the imported proposition carries the companion's corollary on a rate with a linear drift (R1069 there).
% was: timescale.
\end{proposition}
% R1068 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the companion's trend-survivorship proposition imported (R1068 there; his direction of Nov 27).

\begin{theorem}[The pace of survivors; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:pace}
Let the strength's rate and every anatomy rate be driftless Brownian,
independent of one another and of the background, so that nothing in the
law holds a lead up, and let $H_t$ be the histories that survive to $t$
with strength at least one. (i) For every $t$, the law of the path of
each anatomy rate given survival to $t$, or given $H_t$, dominates its
unconditioned law stochastically: the filtering only raises a
technoanatomy. (ii) With $m$ units, $\PP(H_t)\ge c\,t^{-(1+m)/2}$, and
given survival to $t$ the time spent holding strength with a lead no
longer than $(\tfrac12-\delta)\log t$ is at most $t^{1-\delta}$ with
probability tending to one, against a quarter of all time unconditioned.
(iii) Given $H_t$, the lead of each unit at the horizon is at most $w$
with probability at most $C(w+\log\log t)\,t^{-3/2}$: the scalar stands
below the strength with probability $O(t^{-3/2}\log\log t)$, against one
half with no filtering, and the lead is of order $t^{3/2}$ to within any
power of $t$. (iv) At frozen background, survivors lack strength with
probability at most $C't^{-1}\log^3t$ times the ratio
$\PP(T>t/2)/\PP(T>t)$, which is bounded at more than half of the horizons
$2^k$ and at every horizon if survival decays as a power. The proof rests
on the association of a Brownian path, the survival weight being
nondecreasing in the path of each anatomy rate; the survival exponent,
and that survivors hold strength at every horizon, are not part of what
is proved.
\end{theorem}
% R1074 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: his request of Nov 27 for an analytic proof of the outcome the simulation of R1073 showed (the pace of technoanatomy against strength as an output of the filtering). The survivorship companion's theorem on the pace of survivors (R1074 there) imported.

\begin{theorem}[Domains; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:domains}
Let each of $m$ domains carry a credit $x_j$ with a rate of its own, a
strength $x_j^+$ and a scalar $\tau_j$, with total strength
$X=\sum_jx_j^+$ and hazard
$h=B_t\operatorname{sech}^2X+\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2(x_j^+/\tau_j)$; a domain
is developed at strength one or more and idle at or below zero, and
Definition~\ref{imp:model} is the case of fixed shares $x_j=s_jx$. (i)
$\operatorname{sech}^2(\sum_jx_j)=\prod_j\operatorname{sech}^2x_j/E^2$ with
$E=\tfrac12[\prod_j(1+\tanh x_j)+\prod_j(1-\tanh x_j)]$; at nonnegative
strengths $1\le E\le2^{m-1}$, $E=1$ while at most one domain is developed
and $E\to2^{m-1}$ when all are, and the product without the divisor
weakens as a decomposition is refined, $\operatorname{sech}^{2m}(x/m)\to1$.
(ii) A domain whose credit tends to $-\infty$ is idle and its scalar bears
on nothing; a domain whose credit grows must have its technoanatomy keep
ahead, as in Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond}. With independent rates, each
transient or recurrent with nonzero mean,
\[
\PP(\Lambda_\infty<\infty)=\prod_j\bigl(1-V_j+V_jA_j\bigr)-\prod_j\bigl(1-V_j\bigr),
\]
$V_j$ and $A_j$ being the viability functionals of the strength rate and
the anatomy rate of domain $j$: each domain is left idle or is developed
with its technoanatomy ahead, and one at least is developed. (iii) With
every strength rate driftless Brownian, $\Lambda_\infty=\infty$ almost
surely.
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[The pace, domain by domain; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:pacedomains}
With every rate driftless Brownian and independent, the statements of
Theorem~\ref{imp:pace} hold for each domain, with its own strength in
place of the common one and the histories that survive with that domain
developed in place of $H_t$. The filtering raises the technoanatomies
jointly, the domination holding for functionals nondecreasing in the
paths of all the anatomy rates. For a domain idle throughout, the anatomy
rate keeps its unconditioned law given survival. At frozen background the
total strength is below one with probability at most $C't^{-1}\log^3t$
times the ratio $\PP(T>t/2)/\PP(T>t)$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proposition}[The weights of a redundant decomposition; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:weights}
Let the content of the system be a space of finite mass and the
institutions labels, parts $A_j$ of it of mass $m_j$, with $\rho_{jk}$ the
share of $j$'s content that $k$ also reads. (i) If every total overlap
$\sum_{k\neq j}\rho_{jk}$ is below one, the equations
$w_j=1-\sum_{k\neq j}\rho_{jk}w_k$ have a unique solution, in $(0,1]^m$,
the limit at a geometric rate of the iterates from $w=1$. (ii) The
weights minimize the distance in mean square from $\sum_jw_j\one_{A_j}$ to
one, the minimum being the resolution $\varepsilon_{\mathcal S}$ of the
labels; for a density $f$ with readings $F_j=\int_{A_j}f$,
$|\sum_jw_jF_j-\int f|\le\varepsilon_{\mathcal S}\operatorname{dist}(f,\mathcal S)$,
$\mathcal S$ the span of the labels' indicators, so that a density the
labels resolve is integrated exactly. (iii) Two families of labels with
the same span give the same weighted sum of readings for every density.
(iv) The internal hazard, an integral $\int\gamma\sinh^2u$ over the
content, is thereby approximated by $\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2u_j$ with
$\Gamma_j=w_j\hat\Gamma_j$, where $\hat\Gamma_j$ is the amplitude and $u_j$
the load that $j$ reads in the sense of Definition~\ref{gr:def:reading},
and the total strength by $\sum_jw_jx_j^+$. A label that is the common
part of two others has weight $-1$; a negative weight is no amplitude,
and such a label is dropped.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Listening, and one tendency read in parts; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:listening}
(i) Let $\log\tau_j=(1-\kappa)b_j+\kappa\sum_kP_{jk}\log\tau_k$ with
$0\le\kappa<1$ and $P$ a matrix of shares. Then $\log\tau=Kb$ with
$K=(1-\kappa)(I-\kappa P)^{-1}$, whose entries are nonnegative, whose rows
sum to one and for which $K_{jj}\ge1-\kappa$: each scalar is a weighted
geometric mean of the contents $e^{b_k}$, between the least and the
greatest of them, and falls only as the power $K_{jj}$ of its own;
$K_{jk}/(1-\kappa)$ is the expected number of visits to $k$ of the walk
with transitions $P$ from $j$, stopped with probability $1-\kappa$ at each
step. With the contents moved by independent driftless Brownian rates,
Theorem~\ref{imp:pacedomains} holds, the constant of its lead bound
divided by $K_{jj}$. (ii) Let the institutions' rates be the averages over
their contents of one Brownian motion over the content. Each is a
driftless Brownian rate; two of them have correlation
$r_{jk}=\sqrt{\rho_{jk}\rho_{kj}}$; and given a movement $\delta$ of the
rate of $j$, that of $k$ is Gaussian with mean $\rho_{kj}\delta$ and the
share $1-r_{jk}^2$ of its variance.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proposition}[Which channel carries the hazard; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:carry}
At a strength $x>0$ with leads $\ell_j\ge0$, external hazard
$h^{\mathrm{ext}}$ and units $h_j$ of loads $u_j$: (i) the hazard lies
within the factor $4(1+m)$ of the greatest of $Be^{-2x}$ and the
$\Gamma_je^{-2\ell_j}$: it is set by the least margin among the strength
and the leads. (ii) A unit carries more hazard than the external channel
if $\ell_j\le x+\tfrac12\log(\Gamma_j/4B)$ and less if
$\ell_j\ge x+\tfrac12\log(2\Gamma_j/B)$. (iii)
$\partial h/\partial\log\tau_j=-2u_j\coth(u_j)h_j<0$ and
$\partial h/\partial x=-2\tanh(x)h^{\mathrm{ext}}+x^{-1}\sum_j2u_j\coth(u_j)h_j$:
a longer technoanatomy always lowers the hazard, and strength lowers it,
the scalars held, exactly when
$x\tanh(x)\,h^{\mathrm{ext}}>\sum_ju_j\coth(u_j)\,h_j$, at small loads when
the internal hazard is below $x\tanh x$ times the external one; with the
leads held, strength always lowers it.
\end{proposition}
% R1077 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: his messages of Nov 27 (the external hazard as a product over the units' indices; the redundancy of the factors; the amplitudes as a correction reached by a fixed point; the units listening to one another; institutions as probing labels) and his verdict of Nov 27 to take the recommendations of the working note exactly and to finish the proofs and load them into the papers. Five results of the survivorship companion imported (R1077 there): domains, the pace domain by domain, the weights of a redundant decomposition, listening with the one tendency read in parts, and which channel carries the hazard.

\begin{proposition}[Shared loads; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:sharing}
Let the content of the system bear at each point a blend
$\sum_j\pi_ju_j$ of the loads $u_j=x_j^+/\tau_j$, with shares $\pi_j\ge0$
summing to one, and let the \emph{sharing measure} $\mu$ say how much
amplitude bears each blend. The internal hazard is
$h_\mu(u)=\int\sinh^2\langle\pi,u\rangle\,\mu(d\pi)$, with total amplitude
$\Gamma=\mu(\Delta)$, amplitudes $\Gamma_j=\int\pi_j\,d\mu$ and pooled load
$\bar u=\sum_j\Gamma_ju_j/\Gamma$. (i) \emph{Forms.} Mass on the vertices
gives the separate units $\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2u_j$; mass at one blend the
merged unit $\Gamma\sinh^2\bar u$; both together the mixture
$\varphi\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2u_j+(1-\varphi)\Gamma\sinh^2\bar u$; mass at
the rows of a matrix of shares $S$ the pooled units
$\sum_ia_i\sinh^2(Su)_i$; and mass $\nu_Q$ at the barycenter of each face
the powerset form $\sum_Q\nu_Q\sinh^2\bar u_Q$, one term for every set
$Q$ of institutions at the mean load of the set, $\nu_Q$ being the
amplitude of the content read by exactly the labels in $Q$ and
proportional to $p^{|Q|}(1-p)^{m-|Q|}$ where the labels read the content
independently with probability $p$. Split each institution into parts
that carry its load: the powerset forms of such refinements approximate
every $h_\mu$ uniformly on bounded sets of loads. (ii) \emph{Bounds.}
$\Gamma\sinh^2\bar u\le h_\mu\le\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2u_j$, and $h_\mu$ is
nondecreasing in every load. (iii) \emph{One law along the common
direction.} With $D=\sum_j\partial/\partial u_j$,
$D^2h_\mu=4h_\mu+2\Gamma$. (iv) \emph{Sameness.}
$\partial^2h_\mu/\partial u_i\partial u_j=2\int\pi_i\pi_j\cosh(2\langle\pi,u\rangle)\,d\mu\ge0$,
zero exactly when no content bears both loads, and the matrix of these is
nonnegative definite. The \emph{distinctness} of a pair, the second
derivative of $h_\mu$ along the difference of the two loads over that
along their sum, lies in $[0,1]$, is one exactly when the pair shares no
content and zero exactly when the two bear the same share everywhere; in
the mixture of two equal units its odds are
$\frac{\varphi}{1-\varphi}\cosh(u_2-u_1)$. (v) \emph{Limits.} At small
loads $h_\mu=u^{\!\top}Gu+O(|u|^4)$, $G_{ij}=\int\pi_i\pi_j\,d\mu$; at
large loads $\frac1{2t}\log h_\mu(tv)$ tends to the greatest
$\langle\pi,v\rangle$ over the support of $\mu$. (vi) \emph{A rule on the
derivative along a difference.} For two units of amplitude $a$, the
hazard that is the merged unit at equal loads and whose derivative in the
difference $\delta$ of the loads is that of the separate units taken at
the imbalance $f(\delta)$, $0\le f(\delta)\le\delta$, is
$2a\sinh^2\bar u+a\cosh(2\bar u)\int_0^\delta\sinh f$, and it lies between
the merged unit and the separate units; $\sinh f=\varphi\sinh\delta$ is
the mixture. For three or more units linked by pairs, such a rule on
every pair, each from that pair's loads alone, holds only for the
mixture, with one $\varphi$ for all the pairs. (vii) The filtering raises
the technoanatomies jointly under any sharing measure, as in
Theorem~\ref{imp:pacedomains}; the bounds on the leads are not
established under sharing.
\end{proposition}
% R1083 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: his notes of Nov 27 (the internal hazard of two indices fixed by its derivative in the difference of their loads, with a function between zero, same, and the identity, different; the Aethic reading by which products of proper children are valid; whether the rule holds water or the thing should be rethought; a decomposition with one entry for every member of the powerset, and whether it converges on something more organic; whether partial derivatives are still used; and to carry these into the papers). One result of the survivorship companion imported (R1083 there): shared loads, with the forms, the bounds, the law along the common direction, the second derivatives and the distinctness, the limits, the rule on the derivative along a difference, and what holds under the filtering.

\begin{theorem}[The ceiling; \textbf{[P]} in \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}]\label{imp:ceiling}
In the critical regime the survivorship-conditioned law converges, in the
hard-wall model, to the Doob transform by the persistence-harmonic
function, $Q^{\mathrm{hard}}$, proved, with the soft-wall law
$Q^{\mathrm{soft}}$ conditional on the companion's ratio hypothesis;
either is locally absolutely continuous with respect to the
unconditioned law on survivors and globally singular. No finite observation of a surviving path certifies
which measure a record is under.
\end{theorem}

\section{Technostrength and technoanatomy}\label{sec:coords}

\subsection{Technostrength and technoanatomy}\label{gr:sub:coords}

\begin{definition}[The two coordinates]\label{gr:def:coords}
\emph{Technostrength} is the positive part $x_t^+$ of the muffling
exponent: the capacity the biosphere has accumulated, zero while its
credit stands at or below the wall, where its hazard is the bare
background. \emph{Technoanatomy} is the structure that carries that
capacity, taken whole: what the capacities are, how they are organized,
and the tendencies by which that organization changes, so that it is an
Aethus in its own right and its tendency to change belongs to it. The
internal channel of the survivorship companion
\cite{GoldenSurvivorship} reads it through scalars: a unit of the
structure carries the \emph{technoanatomy scalar} $\tau>0$, the scale of
technostrength it can bear, and is loaded in proportion to $x^+/\tau$;
the scalar's logarithm is carried by an \emph{anatomy rate} $\eta$, its
tendency; and the \emph{lead} $\log(\tau/x^+)$ is how far the scalar
stands ahead of the strength. The \emph{sign} of technoanatomy is the
sign of that race, favorable when the scalars outrun the strength and
adverse when they fall behind. It is an outcome and not a primitive: for
a scalar carried as a law of the strength, $\tau=\tau(x)$, a unit's
hazard falls as the strength grows exactly when $d\log\tau/d\log x>1$,
and for a constant anatomy rate the sign is that of $\eta$.

The two are of different kinds, and keeping them apart is the whole of
what follows. Technostrength is a \emph{state coordinate}: it
fluctuates, and by Theorem~\ref{imp:half} a biosphere in the
critical driftless regime, however well constituted, spends exactly half
of logarithmic time with its credit below its initial level. Technoanatomy is a \emph{structural property}: its scalars move only
along tendencies that belong to it, it says what capacity does when
acquired, and an adverse sign names the biosphere whose own strength is
turned against it, the biosphere that eats itself.

\paragraph{Where the two channels came from.} The split into an external and an internal hazard, and the technoanatomy that the second answers to, have one motivating observation behind them, recorded here because it explains why technoanatomy is a structural property and not a state. Once a civilization gains the technological power to destroy or redirect an asteroid on course to wipe out its planet, it has answered one external hazard and, in the same act, created a risk worth answering: that the same technology can be turned back on the biosphere as a weapon. The power that muffles the external channel is the power that opens the internal one. Dialing up technostrength therefore requires an increasingly explicit mechanism not merely for maintaining the biosphere but for maintaining it with stability, an internal hazard rate that is low and robustly low, and that requirement is what technoanatomy meets, its scalar measuring how much strength a unit of the structure can bear and its tendency whether that measure keeps pace. Technoanatomy is then the second half of the same observation: scale a civilization's reach to the whole galaxy while leaving its anatomy invariant, its grade of integration, its species-level self-awareness and initiative, its robust incentive against hostility, and the result is a galactic civilization that self-obliterates on the timescale of literal seconds, every capacity of the enlarged body available to every fracture of the unchanged one. The standing example is an ethics that counsels doing nothing while a continent of one's own species suffers, an ethics regarded here as deeply immoral in its own right; the model's point is narrower and does not depend on that judgment. A civilization that cannot or will not protect one of its own continents has, in the model's terms, a profoundly poor anatomy, and scaling it to the size of the galaxy would be a spontaneous bloodied implosion rather than an expansion. That is why a technoanatomy left behind by its strength is eating oneself, and why no magnitude of $x_t$ substitutes for technoanatomy.

\paragraph{The scalar as an extraction.} Technoanatomy is a structural-qualitative property, and the paper reads its scalar as one extraction from it. A truer rendering would decompose technoanatomy into several scalars or a vector, the grade of integration, self-awareness and initiative, and hostility-avoidance incentive among its coordinates, as of a high-dimensional complex system, and would obtain the internal hazard rate as a function of that vector and of technostrength together. The unit form takes the first step toward that rendering: the internal hazard is a sum of units, one per coordinate, each loaded by the strength in proportion to its own scalar, and \S\ref{gr:sub:reading} gives the analysis of the sum. The second step is a constraint, whether through correlation among the coordinates or otherwise, giving the anatomy system a hard cutoff, a surface past which a coordinate's scalar stops keeping pace and its unit inflates exponentially as a function of technostrength. The result that survives the decomposition is that the weakest unit decides: a vector anatomy with any coordinate that falls behind the strength turns capacity against the biosphere along that coordinate, whatever the others do.
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: the scalar read as an extraction from technoanatomy, the sum of units being the first of his two stepping stones (R1064).
% was: \paragraph{The scalar as an idealization.} A single exponent $\rho$ is the first idealization of a structural-qualitative property, and the paper reads it so. A truer rendering would decompose technoanatomy into several scalars or a vector, the grade of integration, self-awareness and initiative, and hostility-avoidance incentive among its coordinates, as of a high-dimensional complex system, and would obtain the internal hazard rate as a function of that vector and of technostrength together. Two stepping-stone idealizations lie between the scalar and that rendering: an internal hazard written as a sum of several exponentials, one per coordinate, in place of a single one; and an imported constraint, whether through correlation among the coordinates or otherwise, giving the anatomy system a hard cutoff, a surface south of which the internal hazard inflates exponentially as a function of technostrength rather than declining with it. Every result below except those of \S\ref{gr:sub:reading}, which takes the first of the stepping stones, is stated for the scalar, and inherits the idealization; the sign result in particular is the statement that survives the decomposition, since a vector anatomy with any coordinate on the wrong side of its cutoff turns capacity against the biosphere along that coordinate, whatever the others do.

\emph{Adverse strength} is $x<0$, credit below the wall, where the
technostrength is zero; \emph{adverse anatomy} is a technoanatomy whose
scalars fall behind the strength they carry, and only the second is a
defect.
% R1064 (Opus 5.5, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: adverse strength and adverse anatomy in unit form (R1064).
% was: \emph{Adverse strength} is $x<0$; \emph{adverse anatomy} is $\rho<0$,
% was: and only the second is a defect.
\end{definition}

Two results of Section~\ref{sec:imports} are worth restating in this vocabulary because
they are counterintuitive. \emph{Technostrength is not cumulative --- in the critical regime.}
Under a recurrent driver $x$ oscillates on scale $t^{3/2}$ and returns
to every level infinitely often: it is not a stock that accumulates but
a magnitude that fluctuates, so a framework treating it as monotonically
increasing has the wrong kind of object. Under a \emph{transient} driver
it does accumulate, and the ladder picture is locally right --- which is
the concession the broader Golden program makes precise and prices.

\emph{Every biosphere is adversely configured half the time.} By
Theorem~\ref{imp:half} the set $\{t:x_t<0\}$ has logarithmic density
exactly $\tfrac12$, for every parameter value and every biosphere.
Adverse \emph{strength} is therefore not a defect distinguishing some
biospheres from others but a condition all of them occupy half of
logarithmic time --- which is precisely why it cannot be what
``bad anatomy'' means, and why the two must be kept apart.

\emph{A tendency holds for as long as it has held.} By
Proposition~\ref{imp:trend}, the direction in which a driftless rate is
carrying its coordinate, once held for an age $a$, is held for a further
$s$ with probability $(a/(a+s))^{1/2}$. What ends a stretch is a change of
tendency, the strength beginning to fall where it rose or a scalar where
it improved, and the hazard of that change falls as $1/(2a)$. An improving
technoanatomy therefore keeps improving, and a strength that has climbed
keeps climbing, in the Lindy manner and under no Lindy premise: the law
follows from placing the noise on the rate, and its weight
$\lambda^{-1/2}$ over rates of reversal is derived, where
Definition~\ref{gr1:def:aethiclindy} takes its weight as a premise. This
is \emph{trend survivorship}: of the tendencies in progress at a given
time the old are overrepresented, and an old tendency is the one to
expect to continue.
% R1068 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: trend survivorship restated in the paper's vocabulary, beside the two results on technostrength (his phrase of Nov 26 for the Lindy noise on the scalar's rate, and his direction of Nov 27).

\emph{The tendency is the system's.} The tendency that holds is not that
of an institution or of a capacity. It belongs to the Aethus that
describes the techno-system of the biosphere whole, a description that
stands almost entirely above the level of particular institutions, and
the rates $\mu$ and $\eta_j$ are outputs of that system, as its hazard
modes are. What the law of Proposition~\ref{imp:trend} waits on is such an output being
shaken up, the system ceasing to put out the tendency it had. The Aethus
leaves the rate of that event blank, a superposition over rates $\lambda$
each constant in time with the weight $\lambda^{-1/2}$, and the filtering
by the tendency's having held for an age $a$ multiplies each rate by
$e^{-\lambda a}$. What it retains is $\lambda^{-1/2}e^{-\lambda a}$, of
mean $1/(2a)$: the rates left standing are of order $1/a$ and below,
arbitrarily small ones among them. A tendency of the techno-system
therefore survives unchanged for long, the hazard of its being shaken up
standing, after the filtering, at the small values its age has selected;
and the credit, the integral of a rate held in this way, is the
integrated Brownian motion of the model.
% R1074 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: his message of Nov 27 on what the Lindy effect of the tendency is a property of: the Aethus describing the techno-system of the biosphere, close to wholly above its institutions, whose tendencies are its outputs as modes are; the event bounded is such a tendency being shaken up, and under the filtering the rates of that event are arbitrarily small. Paragraph added after the paragraph on the tendency that holds.

\emph{The pace is an output.} A unit's hazard is a function of its lead
alone, $\Gamma\sinh^2(e^{-\ell})$ with $\ell=\log(\tau/x^+)$, so that a
very low internal hazard $\varepsilon$ is a scalar standing above the
strength by the factor $(\Gamma/\varepsilon)^{1/2}$, and a hazard that
stays integrable is a lead that keeps growing: technoanatomy far larger
than technostrength, and still gaining on it, is what a low internal
hazard \emph{is} (the survivorship companion \cite{GoldenSurvivorship}). In the coordinates of strength and lead
the two channels are then two walls, the external one for the strength
and the unit's for its lead, and survival is the keeping of both. Give
the two rates the generic law, driftless and independent, with nothing to
hold a lead up, and condition on survival: the leads of survivors grow as
their strength does, their loads tend to zero, and while a lead is near
its wall their two rates move together. In the companion's run the median lead
falls unconditioned, from $0.07$ at $T=2$ to $-1.94$ at $T=8$, and
rises among survivors, from $0.14$ to $9.70$, the scalar standing
above the strength in $96\%$ of them at $T=8$. The pace of
technoanatomy against strength is therefore not a premise of the model:
it is what the generic law looks like after the filtering. For the
histories that survive holding strength this is proved
(Theorem~\ref{imp:pace}): the filtering only raises a technoanatomy, at every
horizon; the lead of such a survivor is of order $t^{3/2}$ at the horizon
$t$; and its scalar stands below its strength with probability
$O(t^{-3/2}\log\log t)$, against one half with no filtering. The thought
experiment behind the names is one move in these coordinates. Scaling
the strength by a factor $\kappa$ with the structure unaltered lowers
every lead by $\log\kappa$, through its wall once $\kappa$ is large,
where the unit's hazard is $\tfrac14\Gamma\exp(2\kappa e^{-\ell})$
(Theorem~\ref{gr1:thm:squarecube}); and like the constructed graph of
\S\ref{sch:par:constructedgraph}, the civilization scaled to a galaxy
with its anatomy invariant is a state that can be written down and is
not reached from within, a lead that short being held at the horizon by
a vanishing fraction of the histories that survive with strength.
Fiction that joins galactic reach to the instability of present-day
humanity, the wars of \emph{Star Wars} and the warring powers of
\emph{Star Trek}, sets its story in that state, and the model counts
against it twice: the state is not arrived at, and a civilization placed
in it does not last, the hazard of its units standing at
$\tfrac14\Gamma\exp(2\kappa e^{-\ell})$.

\subsection{The anatomy read at a strength}\label{gr:sub:reading}

The first step of Definition~\ref{gr:def:coords}, an internal hazard
carried by several units, already changes what a single scalar can be
asked to carry. Let the internal hazard be borne by finitely many units
with the scalars held fixed,
\begin{equation}\label{gr:eq:modes}
h^{\mathrm{int}}(x)=\sum_i\Gamma_i\sinh^2\!\Big(\frac{x}{\tau_i}\Big),\qquad\Gamma_i>0,\ \tau_i>0,
\end{equation}
at strengths $x>0$. The scalars and the amplitudes are the structure, and
nothing in them depends on the strength. A unit's hazard grows as the
square of its load while the load is small and as $\tfrac14e^{2x/\tau}$
once it is large, so that at a great enough strength the unit of least
scalar carries the largest exponential rate, $2/\tau_i$.

\begin{definition}[The reading]\label{gr:def:reading}
With total amplitude $\Gamma=\sum_i\Gamma_i$ and amplitude shares
$p_i=\Gamma_i/\Gamma$, the scalar \emph{read at} strength $x$ is the scalar
$\hat\tau(x)$ of the single unit of amplitude $\Gamma$ that carries the
same internal hazard, $\Gamma\sinh^2(x/\hat\tau(x))=h^{\mathrm{int}}(x)$:
the load $x/\hat\tau(x)$ is the quasi-arithmetic mean, with generator
$\sinh^2$, of the loads $x/\tau_i$ under the shares $p_i$. The units carry
the shares $w_i(x)=\Gamma_i\sinh^2(x/\tau_i)/h^{\mathrm{int}}(x)$ of the
internal hazard at that strength.
\end{definition}

\begin{proposition}[The reading falls to the weakest unit; \textbf{[P]} given \eqref{gr:eq:modes}]\label{gr:prop:zeromark}
\leavevmode
\begin{enumerate}
\item[(i)] \emph{The reading only falls.} $\hat\tau$ is nonincreasing in
the strength, strictly unless all scalars are equal. At zero strength it
is the root-mean-square reading of the fragilities $1/\tau_i$,
$\hat\tau(0^+)=(\sum_ip_i\tau_i^{-2})^{-1/2}$, and as the strength grows
it tends to the least scalar $\tau_{\min}$, with
$1/\hat\tau(x)=1/\tau_{\min}-\log(\Gamma/\Gamma_{\min})/(2x)+o(1/x)$,
$\Gamma_{\min}$ being the amplitude the units at $\tau_{\min}$ carry.
\item[(ii)] \emph{The weakest unit washes out the others.} For
$\tau_j<\tau_i$ the ratio of shares $w_j/w_i$ increases with the
strength, from $(\Gamma_j/\Gamma_i)(\tau_i/\tau_j)^2$ at zero strength to
infinity, asymptotically as $e^{2x(1/\tau_j-1/\tau_i)}$, and the share of
the weakest units rises to one.
\item[(iii)] \emph{Two units.} For $\tau_2<\tau_1$ and
$\Delta=1/\tau_2-1/\tau_1$, the weaker unit carries less than half of the
internal hazard at strength $x$ exactly when
\begin{equation}\label{gr:eq:zeromark}
\frac{\Gamma_2}{\Gamma_1}<\bigg(\frac{\sinh(x/\tau_1)}{\sinh(x/\tau_2)}\bigg)^{2},
\end{equation}
whose right side falls from $(\tau_2/\tau_1)^2$ at zero strength,
asymptotically as $e^{-2\Delta x}$: the amplitude a weak unit may have
while carrying the lesser share falls exponentially in the strength at
which it is read. If $\Gamma_2/\Gamma_1<(\tau_2/\tau_1)^2$, the weaker unit
takes the greater share past the unique crossing
\begin{equation}\label{gr:eq:crossing}
x^\times=\frac{\log(\Gamma_1/\Gamma_2)}{2\Delta}+o(1)
\qquad(\Gamma_2/\Gamma_1\to0).
\end{equation}
\item[(iv)] \emph{A spread.} If the amplitudes are spread over the
fragilities $c=1/\tau$ with Gaussian weight of mean $\bar c$ and variance
$v$, a unit at $c$ being the unit at $|c|$, then
$h^{\mathrm{int}}(x)=\tfrac{\Gamma}{2}(e^{2vx^2}\cosh(2\bar cx)-1)$, and the
read fragility $1/\hat\tau(x)$ rises from $(\bar c^{\,2}+v)^{1/2}$ at zero
strength to $\bar c+vx$, with exponentially small error: the mark is
$vx$, linear in the strength.
\item[(v)] \emph{Viability.} With the scalars moving, viability holds
exactly when it holds for every unit taken alone with the external
channel, Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond}(ii): the weakest unit decides.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
(i) Write $\psi(v)=\sinh^2v$ and $c_i=1/\tau_i$, so that
$x/\hat\tau(x)=\psi^{-1}(\sum_ip_i\psi(xc_i))$. For $0<x<x'$ put $k=x'/x>1$.
The claim $1/\hat\tau(x')\ge1/\hat\tau(x)$ compares the quasi-arithmetic
means of the $c_i$ with generators $\psi(x'\,\cdot)$ and $\psi(x\,\cdot)$,
and by the comparison theorem for such means (Hardy, Littlewood and
P\'olya, \emph{Inequalities}, Chapter~III) it holds for all inputs when
$\chi(s)=\psi(k\,\psi^{-1}(s))$ is convex. With $\cosh2v=1+2s$,
$\chi(s)=\tfrac12(T_k(1+2s)-1)$, where $T_k(y)=\cosh(k\operatorname{arcosh}y)$,
and for $y=\cosh\theta$, $\theta>0$,
$T_k''(y)=k\bigl(k\cosh(k\theta)\sinh\theta-\sinh(k\theta)\cosh\theta\bigr)/\sinh^3\theta>0$,
since $\tanh(k\theta)<k\tanh\theta$ for $k>1$; the convexity is strict. The
limits follow from $\psi(v)=v^2(1+O(v^2))$ at zero and
$\psi(v)=\tfrac14e^{2v}(1+O(e^{-2v}))$ at infinity. (ii)
$w_j/w_i=(\Gamma_j/\Gamma_i)(\sinh(c_jx)/\sinh(c_ix))^2$ with $c_j>c_i$, and
the logarithmic derivative of the ratio of sines is
$c_j\coth(c_jx)-c_i\coth(c_ix)>0$, since $c\mapsto c\coth(cx)$ is
increasing, its derivative in $c$ being
$(\tfrac12\sinh(2cx)-cx)/\sinh^2(cx)>0$. (iii) is (ii) for two units, the
weaker unit's share being one half exactly at equality in
\eqref{gr:eq:zeromark}; at large strength its right side is
$e^{-2\Delta x}(1+O(e^{-2x/\tau_1}))$. (iv)
$\sinh^2(xc)=\tfrac12(\cosh(2xc)-1)$ and the Gaussian moment generating
function gives $\E\cosh(2xc)=e^{2vx^2}\cosh(2\bar cx)$; at large strength
$x/\hat\tau(x)=vx^2+\bar cx+O(e^{-4\bar cx})$. (v) The cumulative hazard is
the external channel's integral plus the units' integrals, and is finite
exactly when each is.
\end{proof}

With $\tau_1=5$, $\tau_2=1$ and the weaker unit's amplitude a thousandth of
the sturdier unit's, the reading is $4.94$ at zero strength, $2.96$ at
$x=5$, $1.53$ at $x=10$, $1.21$ at $x=20$ and $1.09$ at $x=40$, falling
toward the weaker unit's $1$. The weaker unit takes the greater share
past $x^\times=4.04$; at a millionth, past $8.59$. Each factor of a
thousand taken out of the weak amplitude moves the crossing by about
$\log(1000)/(2\Delta)=4.3$, which is logarithmic, so no reduction short of
removal puts the crossing out of the strength's reach.

Definition~\ref{gr:def:coords} calls technoanatomy a structural property,
and for the sum that stands: the scalars and amplitudes are held fixed
here, and the reading moves with the strength by no change of theirs.
What the sum removes is the identification of a structure's technoanatomy
with the scalar read at the strength it happens to have. A structure with
a weak unit reads near its sturdy units wherever the weak unit's share is
small, and the same structure, nothing in it altered, is read by its weak
unit past $x^\times$. A technoanatomy good enough at one strength is held,
by \eqref{gr:eq:zeromark}, to an exponentially tighter standard at a
greater one. At fixed scalars every reading at positive strength is
adverse, $-\partial_x\log h^{\mathrm{int}}<0$, approaching
$-2/\tau_{\min}$; a favorable reading needs the scalars to grow with the
strength faster than proportionally, which is the race of
Definition~\ref{gr:def:coords}.

This is the square--cube law read across the units.
Theorem~\ref{gr1:thm:squarecube} takes a structure whose technoanatomy is
held and scales its strength. Here the structure is sound at the strength
it has, as an ant is sound at the size of an ant, and the reading belongs
to the structure and the strength together, as the load a frame can bear
belongs to its proportions and its size together: scaled with nothing in
it altered, it is read at the end by its weakest unit. The theorem's bound
holds throughout with the weak unit's own constants, $\Gamma_2$ and
$\tau_2$: below $x^\times$ it is slack, and past $x^\times$ it is the
hazard.

By (ii) the unit that decides is almost absent from what is read. At a
strength below $x^\times$ the reading is carried by the sturdier units,
whose shares the strength is retiring, and a reading near theirs over a
range of strengths bounds the weak amplitude from above, by
\eqref{gr:eq:zeromark} at the top of the range, without excluding it. The
observation behind the two channels has this form: before anyone can
redirect an asteroid, the turning of that capacity on the biosphere is a
unit of the internal hazard with a negligible share, and its share grows
with the strength that makes the capacity usable.

Two standards follow, and they differ in kind. Over strengths bounded by
some $x_{\max}$, a reading carried by the sturdier units throughout asks
only that $x^\times>x_{\max}$: a ceiling on the weak amplitude, and not its
absence. The viability event of Definition~\ref{imp:goldenpoint} sets no
such bound, the external channel being driven to zero only as the
strength grows without limit, and by (v) it asks that every unit keep
pace, the weakest included: a weak unit is no defect if its scalar
outruns the strength, and the sturdiest is one if its scalar is held. The
tree of hazard modes in the broader Golden program offers a second way
for a weak unit to cease to count: there a mode is individuated only by
the records its own events leave, and a weak mode whose content lies
below a separatrix dissolves into the sturdier mode above it and decays at
that mode's rate, while above the separatrix it stays resolved and (ii)
applies. On that program's worked tree, computed for its modes in
exponential form, a weak mode started just above its separatrix gives an
exponent read at $+0.80$ at the base point and a crossing at $14.5$;
started just below, it gives the same $+0.80$ and no crossing, and the
reading at a low strength does not tell the two apart. The sum takes its
amplitudes as given at the base point; a law for them, and for whether a
weak unit of an actual biosphere can be brought below such a cutoff, is
open \textbf{[O]}.

His proposal of Nov 14: that the sum of hazards be read so that the zero mark of technoanatomy shifts with the scale of technostrength, an anatomy good enough at a low strength reading adverse at a higher one, as the square-cube analogy directly. Stated for a bare sum of modes, with the lane-tree numbers of the Golden master (its Table ln:tab:zeromark) quoted for the cutoff. His sign (negative favorable) is that of alpha - beta. The asteroid sentence is a reading of his two-channels paragraph in the sum's terms. Whether the Golden Point is met at a finite strength is not asserted either way.


\subsection{Institutions: the decomposition nobody chooses}\label{gr:sub:institutions}

The units of \eqref{gr:eq:modes} were taken as given. They are not given:
the techno-system is one Aethus, and nothing in it marks where one unit
ends and the next begins. An \emph{institution} is here a probing label,
an index attached to a concrete thing through which a part of the system
is read; a scalar $\tau_j$, a strength $x_j$ and an amplitude $\Gamma_j$
are what the label $j$ reads. The idealized case is that of labels that
are not redundant with one another, in which the system factors into
Aethae $j$, each advantage $x_j$ balanced by its own technoanatomy and
each sweeping away its part of the external hazard: the model with
domains (Theorem~\ref{imp:domains}), whose internal channel is the sum of the domains'
units and whose external channel is the product of their mufflings up to
one symmetric factor (Theorem~\ref{imp:domains}(i)). Labels attached to concrete things
are never free of redundancy. What one reads another reads in part, and
no choice of labels removes this: it is a limit on the resolution of
labels, and it has a measure, the distance $\varepsilon_{\mathcal S}$ of
Proposition~\ref{imp:weights}.

\emph{The amplitudes are weights.} Which split of the internal hazard
into units is the natural one has no answer and needs none. Read the
hazard as an integral over the content of the system. A decomposition
into institutions is then a quadrature rule for it, and the amplitudes
$\Gamma_j$ are its weights (Proposition~\ref{imp:weights}). They carry the dependence on the
split, so that the hazard does not: two families of labels that resolve
the same content give the same hazard. With redundant labels the weights
are reached from the idealized case by the fixed point
$w_j=1-\sum_{k\neq j}\rho_{jk}w_k$, each amplitude damped by its overlaps
and the damping corrected in turn, which converges when every label's
total overlap is below one, and the faster the nearer the labels are to
non-redundant. The amplitude shares of Definition~\ref{gr:def:reading}
are these weights, normalized.

\emph{Listening.} The scalar an institution reads is not its own content
alone. It listens: its scalar is a mixture of its own content and of what
the others read, in the shares $1-\kappa$ and $\kappa$, and since the
others listen as well the readings are a fixed point, each a weighted
geometric mean of all the contents (Proposition~\ref{imp:listening}). The two halves of the
phenomenology of a unit that has become very good follow. Its reading no
longer follows its own content, the weight of that content in the mean
being below one and the rest being what it hears from the adjacent
layers. And what it hears is a buffer, which keeps a scalar above zero
when its own content fails and cannot raise it above the greatest content
among those it listens to: the buffer is there only while the rest of the
system holds it up. The weights of the mean are the Green's function of a
walk on the graph of institutions, stopped at each step with probability
$1-\kappa$, and the reading at $j$ is the potential at $j$ of the
contents taken as sources.

\emph{A perturbation is felt, and determines nothing.} Let the
tendencies of the institutions be readings of one tendency of the whole
system (Proposition~\ref{imp:listening}(ii)). Two institutions' tendencies are then correlated by
exactly their overlap $r$, and a push $\delta$ on one moves the other by
$r\delta$ in the mean, each measured in its own spread, leaving the share
$1-r^2$ of its variance where it was. No institution is in a vacuum,
however far apart two of them appear, and none determines another. What
reaches the second institution is not a cause in the sense of exact
determination. It is a re-weighting of its superposition, a filtering
artifact of size $r$, small where the overlap is small and never zero.


\emph{Same and different.} Two institutions may be nearly one thing or
quite distinct, and the internal hazard has to say which. If they are
distinct, each bears its own load, and an imbalance between them costs
what it costs two separate units. If they are the same, only their pooled
load counts, and an imbalance between them costs nothing. The ground
between is that of an Aethus whose factors are not independent. Take two
factors $A_i$ and $A_j$ of the techno-system, each a superposition over
its proper children. The product $A_iA_j$ distributes over the children,
and each product of a child of one with a child of the other is valid or
invalid. Where every such product is valid the two factors are different.
Where a product is valid only for matching children they are the same.
Between the two a child of $A_i$ excludes some children of $A_j$ and
leaves the rest, which is implication without parenthood: neither factor
is a parent of the other, and a child of one still prunes the children of
the other. In the content of Proposition~\ref{imp:weights}, with a factor read as the
fillings of the content its label covers, a product of two children is
valid exactly when the two agree on the content both labels read, and the
overlap of the labels is the logarithm of the number of such products
over the number that are valid. The structure is defined for any two
factors of the system. No list of indices is presupposed by it.

\emph{One form for the ground between.} Let each part of the content
bear a blend of the institutions' loads, in shares that sum to one. The
internal hazard is the integral of $\sinh^2$ of the blended load against
the measure that says how much of the content bears each blend, the
\emph{sharing measure} (Proposition~\ref{imp:sharing}). Separate units are the case in which
every part bears one institution's load, and a single merged unit the
case in which every part bears the same blend. A share $\varphi$ of every
unit standing apart and the rest standing as one,
$\varphi\sum_j\Gamma_j\sinh^2u_j+(1-\varphi)\,\Gamma\sinh^2\bar u$, is a
third member. A fourth is the expansion with one term for every set of
institutions, each at the mean load of the set. That expansion is the
powerset of the labels written out: its coefficients are the amplitudes
of the content read by exactly each set, they are binomial where the
labels read the content independently, and under refinement of the labels
it reaches every sharing measure (Proposition~\ref{imp:sharing}(i)). The expansion is therefore
not a correction laid over a description by parts. It is the measure
itself at a finite resolution, as an agreeing state is the sum, over the
powerset of its pins, of the irreducible agreeing superpositions
\cite{Aethus}. Whatever the measure, the hazard lies between that of the
merged unit and that of the separate units, and falls as any scalar
rises, so that the filtering raises the technoanatomies jointly as before
(Proposition~\ref{imp:sharing}(ii), (vii)).

\emph{What the derivatives say.} Every member of the family obeys one
law and differs from the others in one place. Along the common
direction, all the loads raised together, the hazard moves exactly as a
single unit's does: its second derivative there is four times itself
plus twice the total amplitude (Proposition~\ref{imp:sharing}). Across that direction lies
what distinguishes one system from another. The matrix of second
derivatives of the hazard in the loads has no negative entry, and its
entry for two institutions vanishes exactly when they share nothing. For
a pair, the curvature along the difference of the two loads over the
curvature along their sum is a number between zero and one, zero when the
two are the same and one when they are different: the \emph{distinctness}
of the pair. It is not a constant of the pair. In the mixture its odds
are $\varphi/(1-\varphi)$ times the hyperbolic cosine of the imbalance,
so that two institutions that are nearly one while evenly loaded come
apart as one is loaded beyond the other, and at great loads the hazard is
carried by whatever content is least shared and most loaded. At small
loads the hazard is a quadratic form, and a matrix of pairs is the whole
of it. A rule that fixes the first derivative along a pair's difference
from that pair's loads alone, the separate units' derivative taken at a
reduced imbalance, is exact for two institutions and gives the whole
family between one unit and two. For three or more it leaves one number
free for the system, the share $\varphi$ of the mixture (Proposition~\ref{imp:sharing}(vi)):
the response of a pair to its own imbalance depends on the loads of every
institution that shares with either. Which measure the system has is a
proposal about the system, and none has been run against survival.
% R1083 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: his notes of Nov 27 (the internal hazard of two indices fixed by its derivative in the difference of their loads, with a function between zero, same, and the identity, different; the Aethic reading by which products of proper children are valid; whether the rule holds water or the thing should be rethought; a decomposition with one entry for every member of the powerset, and whether it converges on something more organic; whether partial derivatives are still used; and to carry these into the papers). Three paragraphs in the subsection on institutions, before the one on adversivity: same and different as the validity of the products of two factors' children, implication without parenthood, the overlap as the logarithm of all products over valid ones; the sharing measure as one form for the ground between, with the powerset expansion as the measure at a finite resolution; and what the derivatives say, the law along the common direction, the distinctness of a pair, and the rule on the first derivative, exact for two institutions and leaving one number free for three or more.

\emph{Adversivity.} The broader Golden program carries a model, there called
adversivity, of institutions as the nodes of a graph with signed weights
$w_{ij}$ between them and a finite budget of attention for each,
$\sum_{j\neq i}|w_{ij}|=\beta$. With $P=|W|/\beta$ the budget is exactly
what makes $P$ a matrix of shares, and so exactly what bounds the
feedback of the listening. This is a candidate for the step between that
model and technoanatomy, the balance fixing who listens to whom and the
listening fixing each institution's scalar. It is a proposal, and is not
derived.
% R1077 (Fable 5.2, Nov 27 2026), at the author's direction: his messages of Nov 27 (the external hazard as a product over the units' indices; the redundancy of the factors; the amplitudes as a correction reached by a fixed point; the units listening to one another; institutions as probing labels) and his verdict of Nov 27 to take the recommendations of the working note exactly and to finish the proofs and load them into the papers. New subsection after the anatomy read at a strength: institutions as probing labels, the non-redundant idealization and the model with domains, the amplitudes as quadrature weights with their fixed point, the listening law with its buffer and its bound, a perturbation as a re-weighting and not a determination, and the adversivity budget as what bounds the listening (a proposal).

\subsection{What conditioning does to each coordinate}
\label{gr:subsub:measured}

\paragraph{Numerical methods.} All figures reported in this subsection and
in \S\ref{gr:subsub:anatsel} were produced by direct Monte Carlo on the
two-channel model, by forward Euler at step $\Delta=10^{-3}$ for the
driver, the credit and the background exponentials, on $N=10^{6}$ paths.
Conditioning on $\{T>t\}$ is by survival weights: each path carries the
weight $e^{-\Lambda_t}$, which reproduces $\PP(\cdot\mid T>t)$ exactly, so
the conditioned estimates are $\PP^{(T)}$ estimates and not $Q$ estimates,
exactly as the column headings below say. Standard errors are governed by
the effective number of survivors, $(\sum w)^2/\sum w^2$ over the weights
$w$, and not by the number of paths; in the unit-form runs it lies between
$8.8\times10^{4}$ and $2.6\times10^{5}$ at the horizons reported, so that
every conditional probability of those runs carries a standard error below
$10^{-3}$. \emph{Every $\PP(\eta>0\mid\text{survived})$
figure is prior-dependent}: $\eta\sim\mathrm{Unif}(-1,3)$ was chosen to
place a quarter of the mass on a technoanatomy that falls behind and has
no other justification, and the movement of that probability should be
read as the direction and pace of selection under that prior rather than
as a calibrated number. Code and seeds are available on request.

Two experiments are reported, and they are distinct. Both use $\sigma=1$,
$\epsilon=0.05$, $n=3$, $x_0=\mu_0=0$, and one unit of amplitude
$\Gamma=ne^{-6}$ whose scalar starts at $\tau_0=1$ and moves at a constant
anatomy rate, $\tau_t=e^{\eta t}$, the degenerate case of
Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond}(iii). The strength rows below are from the run
at fixed $\eta=2.5$, conditioning horizon $T=4$. The anatomy rows are from
a separate run in which $\eta$ is drawn from the prior
$\mathrm{Unif}(-1,3)$ per path and everything else is as above.

\begin{center}\small
\begin{tabular}{@{}lrr@{}}
\toprule
& General ($\PP$) & Conditioned ($\PP^{(4)}$)\\
\midrule
$\PP(x<0)$ (adverse strength) & $0.500$ & $0.0017$\\
median $x/\sigma T^{3/2}$ & $0.000$ & $+0.785$\\
$\PP(\eta>0)$, $\eta\sim\mathrm{Unif}(-1,3)$ & $0.751$ & $0.997$\\
$5\%$ quantile of $\eta$ & $-0.80$ & $+0.33$\\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{center}

\noindent The two $\eta$ rows depend on the prior $\mathrm{Unif}(-1,3)$
placed on the anatomy rate, which is a choice and not a result; the $x$
rows do not.

\begin{remark}[Truncation versus rescaling]\label{gr:rem:truncresc}
Two numbers, and they differ in kind rather than in force. Adverse
strength is suppressed by a factor of about $290$ --- abruptly, and
within a few multiples of the intrinsic timescale. Technoanatomy is
selected as abruptly: $\PP(\eta>0)$ moves from $0.75$ to $0.997$ at the
same horizon and to $0.9998$ by $T=6$, and the $5\%$ quantile of the
survivors' rate passes zero ($-0.80\to-0.34\to+0.33\to+0.40$ at
$T=0,2,4,6$).

The difference in kind is in what each suppression does next. Strength is
a state coordinate, so conditioning pushes it out of a half-space it can
and does re-enter: the conditioned $\PP(x<0)$ is $0.125$ at $T=2$,
$0.0017$ at $T=4$ and $0.0015$ at $T=6$, nearly level between the last
two. The anatomy's tendency is a structural parameter, selected through
the accumulated hazard cost of the trend it sets, and what is removed does
not return: the conditioned mass on $\eta\le0$ is $0.117$, $0.0029$ and
$0.00017$ at the same horizons, falling seventeenfold between the last
two. Anatomy is the more decisive:
by Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond}, adverse anatomy is eventually fatal
with certainty, while adverse strength is a condition a biosphere in the critical
recurrent regime occupies half of logarithmic time and survives.

\end{remark}

\subsection{Does conditioning select technoanatomy?}
\label{gr:subsub:anatsel}

The measurement above concerns technostrength, a state coordinate; the
claim the argument needs is about technoanatomy's tendency, a structural parameter,
which therefore cannot be selected \emph{within} a trajectory. It can be
selected across an ensemble, and that is directly computable: place a
prior on it, evolve, and reweight by survival. In the run reported the
tendency is a constant anatomy rate $\eta$, with
$\eta\sim\mathrm{Uniform}(-1,3)$, so that $\PP(\eta>0)=0.75$ a priori:

\begin{center}\small
\begin{tabular}{@{}rrrrr@{}}
\toprule
$T$ & $\PP(\text{survive})$ & $\PP(\eta>0\mid\text{survived})$ &
$5\%$ quantile of $\eta$ & median of $\eta$\\
\midrule
$2$ & $8.7\times10^{-3}$ & $0.883$ & $-0.34$ & $1.32$\\
$4$ & $4.8\times10^{-3}$ & $0.997$ & $+0.33$ & $1.61$\\
$6$ & $4.4\times10^{-3}$ & $0.9998$ & $+0.40$ & $1.65$\\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{center}

\noindent Survivorship selects for a technoanatomy that outruns its
strength, and at these horizons it selects past the sign: the survivors'
rates stand clear of zero, their $5\%$ quantile at $+0.33$ and $+0.40$ and
their median at $1.61$ and $1.65$ against the prior's $1.0$. The margin is
the model's and not the prior's. The strength that muffles the background
is the strength that loads the unit; the survivors' median strength has
reached about $6$ at $T=4$ and $10$ at $T=6$; and a scalar $e^{\eta T}$
that has not kept ahead of it is charged $\Gamma\sinh^2$ of the shortfall,
so that a rate which is positive but small has not yet outrun the
strength: the race is decided by $\eta-\mu/x$
(Definition~\ref{gr:def:coords}), and the strength's own relative rate
$\mu/x$ is of order $3/(2T)$ on survivors. The sign criterion of
Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond} is the limit of this margin, which on the
scaling picture closes as $\log T/T$. Survivors
are found with their technoanatomy far ahead of their strength, at the
median rate a scalar near $e^{6.4}$ against a strength near $6$ at $T=4$:
the coupled form of Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond}(iv), a scalar well ahead of
the strength and still improving, is where conditioning puts them.

A technoanatomy locked to its strength is selected otherwise. In the
locked case of Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond}(v), run as the internal channel
$\Gamma e^{-\rho x}$ with a constant gain $\rho\sim\mathrm{Unif}(-1,3)$
and the logistic muffling factor $(1+e^{-b})/(1+e^{x-b})$ at $b=3$, the
other parameters as above, the same experiment gives
$\PP(\rho>0\mid\text{survived})=0.747$, $0.844$ and $0.913$ at
$T=2,4,6$, with $5\%$ quantile $-0.80$, $-0.43$ and $-0.14$ and
effective survivors numbering $8.2\times10^{5}$, $5.1\times10^{4}$ and $3.9\times10^{4}$: selection
on the same sign, and \emph{gradual}. The two priors are alike in form
and put different questions. A locked technoanatomy moves with its
strength at no cost of its own: at $\rho=0$ its scalar grows in proportion
to the strength and its load stays where it was, and the prior asks only
whether the lead widens or narrows. A technoanatomy with a tendency of its
own must do the moving: at $\eta=0$ its scalar stands still while the
strength climbs, which in the locked case's terms is the gain
$-2/\tau$, below every value the locked prior holds, and at $\eta<0$ its
load grows exponentially in time whatever the strength does. Conditioning
that is gradual on whether a lead widens is abrupt on whether a scalar
keeps up at all. Anatomy is the more
decisive of the two coordinates: by Theorem~\ref{imp:twocond} an adverse technoanatomy is
eventually fatal with certainty, while adverse strength is a condition
a biosphere in the critical regime occupies half of logarithmic time and
survives.

\paragraph{The coordinates stay separable.}\label{gr:rem:separable}
Strength and anatomy cannot fuse under conditioning, and the reason is
structural rather than measured: the anatomy's tendency is a parameter of
the model, or a driver of its own, and $x$ a functional of the strength's
